# Shared UI support test cleanup — October 2026

Scope: 77 baseline files; 627 named declarations plus three standalone app-config scenarios. Every assigned test was read alongside its production owner and relevant wire/schema/design/history. Parameterized declarations are one row, as permitted; this is a declaration count, not an invented runtime subtest count.

Disposition decisions were made from behavior and owner inspection; the inventory/export script records them. Search patterns and historical ledgers were not deletion verdicts.

## Shared retention evidence

For each surviving row below, the independent source is listed per file. Its specific regression and assertion excerpts identify what was reviewed; each rewrite/consolidation reason states the final surviving oracle. Excerpts under rewritten/removed declarations intentionally show the baseline assertions, including the coupling being removed. The seam is the production-consumed module API, real storage/RegExp boundary, API/event payload, browser ABI or command boundary named below. Expected data are fixtures/literals anchored to those contracts, not generated from implementation. Internal helper names, algorithms and representations may change while these outputs remain stable. Unique protection lies in the exact client decision/identity/race/invalid-input variant in the row; server/provider parsing does not prove UI scoping, pending-command gates or client cache convergence. Duplicated owners and private identity checks removed in this patch are called out explicitly.

Store request logs in retained rows verify caller-visible REST request semantics (correct identity/revision, coalescing, cancellation, refetch after invalidation), alongside resulting state; they do not assert arbitrary collaborator order. Cases checking copy/error strings retain supplied user/daemon data or required units/state, and rewrites remove incidental sentence expectations.

## Changes and support removal

- Consolidate six feature app-routing files and the daemon-stop route assertion into lib/app-wiring.test.ts. Their individual contract assertions remain. Share one app-store import, event fixture and deterministic event-loop drain; feature-local before/after hooks retain isolation.
- Convert the three standalone app-config scenarios from the sequential .check.ts tail to normal node:test cases. No gate is removed.
- Delete unsupported random diversity, impossible-random-source, fake-only reset, derived-query oracle, private map reuse, microtask scheduling and prose-only cases.
- Consolidate latch tests into emitted-byte/session behavior. Make DOUBLE_TAP_MS, LATCH_OFF, tapLatch and consumeLatch private after repository-wide searches found no external non-test callers.
- Remove theme.test.ts CSS parser helpers and the optional htmlRenderThemeTokens reader parameter. API palette tests own the public mapping/fallback contract.
- Replace cache-TTL sentence snapshots with numerical/state oracles (the selected TTL stays anchored to its current-choice label so the comparison’s own “1h” cannot satisfy it); remove its optional date formatter parameter (only tests supplied it). Remove fixture self-checks and duplicate letter assertions.
- No fixture files, snapshots, dependencies or manifests became unused in this scope. Shared boxes/knowledge fixtures and browser fakes remain used by surviving tests.

## Per-file and per-case evidence

### packages/ui/src/lib/agent-auth-notice.test.ts

Independent source: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-21-agent-chat-gui-design.md §6/§7 and README.md session/composer/mobile workflows.

Public client decisions for the named scenario: active session ownership, overlay key containment, model selection, draft deliveries, remembered preferences or unread completion state. Literal session/model/message/time outputs anchor expected behavior; no test asserts private helper calls.

Observed non-test consumers (module import search): `packages/ui/src/components/status/AgentAuthErrorToast.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/store/app.ts`.

Six-bar assessment for surviving rows: (1) source above independently establishes behavior; (2) the row names a concrete displayed/state/command/protocol failure; (3) assertion witnesses contain independent literal data or an independent browser/RegExp protocol oracle; (4) the named public boundary is observed; (5) no retained test depends on source spelling, private call structure or incidental map reuse; (6) the row isolates a distinct input identity/race/state contract and has no stronger owner proving that client outcome. Non-test callers are the adjoining feature hooks/components and, for routing, store/app.ts; source imports and repository-wide seam searches were inspected. Risk: low for removed junk; state, security and race assertions remain. Validation: normal UI node:test runner with existing preload hooks; whole repository gates are recorded by the root report.

| Exact baseline declaration / scenario | Disposition | Specific regression, oracle, or surviving coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <code>a dismissal sticks across the re-publish the provider load causes</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: a dismissal sticks across the re-publish the provider load causes. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal(shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice(claude, dismissed), false)</code> <code>assert.equal(shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice(claude, dismissed), false)</code> |
| <code>a DIFFERENT message on the same provider still gets through</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: a DIFFERENT message on the same provider still gets through. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal( shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice({ ...claude, message: "Refresh token revoked." }, dismissed), true )</code> |
| <code>the same message on a different provider still gets through</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: the same message on a different provider still gets through. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal( shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice({ ...claude, sessionId: "provider:codex" }, dismissed), true )</code> |
| <code>the key spans [adapterId, status, auth.status, message] (T3's banner key)</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: the key spans [adapterId, status, auth.status, message] (T3's banner key). Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal( shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice(errored, dismissed), false, "the same result never re-toasts" )</code> <code>assert.equal( shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice({ ...errored, authStatus: "unauthenticated" }, dismissed), true, "an ambiguity turning into a verdict is new news" )</code> <code>assert.equal( shouldRaiseAgentAuthNotice({ ...errored, providerStatus: "degraded" }, dismissed), true )</code> |

### packages/ui/src/lib/agent-profile/app-wiring.test.ts

Independent source: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-agent-profile-design.md §7.2 and AGENTS.md wire-validation/persisted-shape rules.

Agent config snapshot, revision-aware load/mutation/event and localStorage preference interfaces. The daemon owns file state; this client must not cross agents/connections or let stale reads replace newer mutations.

Six-bar assessment for surviving rows: (1) source above independently establishes behavior; (2) the row names a concrete displayed/state/command/protocol failure; (3) assertion witnesses contain independent literal data or an independent browser/RegExp protocol oracle; (4) the named public boundary is observed; (5) no retained test depends on source spelling, private call structure or incidental map reuse; (6) the row isolates a distinct input identity/race/state contract and has no stronger owner proving that client outcome. Non-test callers are the adjoining feature hooks/components and, for routing, store/app.ts; source imports and repository-wide seam searches were inspected. Risk: low for removed junk; state, security and race assertions remain. Validation: normal UI node:test runner with existing preload hooks; whole repository gates are recorded by the root report.

| Exact baseline declaration / scenario | Disposition | Specific regression, oracle, or surviving coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <code>agentProfile.changed on the agent-profile channel refetches the loaded agent</code> | OPTIMIZE | Same public event/plugin/tab assertions now run in lib/app-wiring.test.ts, sharing app-store initialization. Feature state remains reset by scoped hooks; channel isolation is still asserted. agentProfile.changed on the agent-profile channel refetches the loaded agent. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.deepEqual(api.gets, ["claude", "claude"])</code> <code>assert.equal(agentProfileStore.getState().agents.claude.snapshot?.revision, "r2")</code> |
| <code>the same message on another channel does not</code> | OPTIMIZE | Same public event/plugin/tab assertions now run in lib/app-wiring.test.ts, sharing app-store initialization. Feature state remains reset by scoped hooks; channel isolation is still asserted. the same message on another channel does not. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.deepEqual(api.gets, ["claude"])</code> |

| <code>stays inside [0, ceiling] for any random source</code> | DELETE | Invalid random-provider outputs have no production caller; random-range sampling duplicates the deterministic full-jitter endpoint/midpoint oracle (bars 1/6). |

| <code>runs after the delay, once</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: runs after the delay, once. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.deepEqual(runs, [])</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(runs, [false])</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(runs, [false])</code> |
| <code>a wake fires every pending retry at once and says so</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: a wake fires every pending retry at once and says so. | <code>a cancelled retry never runs</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: a cancelled retry never runs. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal(ran, false)</code> |
| <code>a retry scheduled during a wake waits for the next one</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: a retry scheduled during a wake waits for the next one. | Exact baseline declaration / scenario | Disposition | Specific regression, oracle, or surviving coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <code>never resolves a prototype member: an extension like &#96;constructor&#96; is the text fallback</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: never resolves a prototype member: an extension like &#96;constructor&#96; is the text fallback. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("notes.constructor"), { kind: "text", mime: "text/plain" })</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("x.__proto__"), { kind: "text", mime: "text/plain" })</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("x.CONSTRUCTOR"), { kind: "text", mime: "text/plain" })</code> |
| <code>classifies by lowercased extension and collapses .tar.* names</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: classifies by lowercased extension and collapses .tar.* names. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("shot.PNG"), { kind: "image", mime: "image/png" })</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("bundle.tar.gz"), { kind: "archive", mime: "application/gzip" })</code> <code>assert.deepEqual(detectFileKind("README"), { kind: "text", mime: "text/plain" })</code> |

Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal(promptRewindTarget({ prompt: indexOnly, facts: facts({}), rollbackSupported: true }), 2)</code> <code>assert.equal( promptRewindTarget({ prompt: { ...indexOnly, indexRewindable: false }, facts: facts({}), rollbackSupported: true }), null )</code> <code>assert.equal( promptRewindTarget({ prompt: indexOnly, facts: facts({}, stamp(40)), rollbackSupported: true }), null, "a compaction the chat holds after it" )</code> |

| <code>brings an older prompt in first, then reads its count off the fresh rows</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: brings an older prompt in first, then reads its count off the fresh rows. ### packages/ui/src/lib/prompt-history/thread-inputs.test.ts

Independent source: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-21-agent-chat-gui-design.md §5.4/§5.5 and docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-thread-index-and-lazy-boot-design.md.

Prompt/checkpoint/history index interfaces and the rewind command boundary. A stale, busy, unsupported or post-compaction target must never post a destructive conversation rewind; index rows and live rows have different authority.

### packages/ui/src/lib/regexp.test.ts

Independent source: ECMAScript RegExp literal/SyntaxCharacter contract; production gu regex consumers in composer, timeline and HTML preview.

The real RegExp engine must match literal metacharacters without interpreting dots or producing invalid Unicode-mode escapes. The oracle is literal text and the independent built-in engine, not the escaping algorithm.

Observed non-test consumers (module import search): `packages/ui/src/components/agent-chat/composer/composer-files.ts`, `packages/ui/src/components/agent-chat/timeline/row-chrome.ts`, `packages/ui/src/lib/html-preview.ts`.

Non-test callers are the adjoining feature hooks/components and, for routing, store/app.ts; source imports and repository-wide seam searches were inspected. Risk: low for removed junk; state, security and race assertions remain. Validation: normal UI node:test runner with existing preload hooks; whole repository gates are recorded by the root report.

| Exact baseline declaration / scenario | Disposition | Specific regression, oracle, or surviving coverage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| <code>escapes every metacharacter so the escaped form matches the literal</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: escapes every metacharacter so the escaped form matches the literal. Baseline assertion evidence: <code>assert.equal(new RegExp(&#96;^(?:${escapeRegExp(literal)})$&#96;, "u").test(literal), true)</code> <code>assert.equal(new RegExp(escapeRegExp("a.b")).test("axb"), false)</code> |

| <code>store: tapping on another session starts that session fresh</code> | KEEP | Regression protected: store: tapping on another session starts that session fresh. ## Counts and validation status

DELETE: 10; REWRITE: 19; OPTIMIZE: 25; CONSOLIDATE: 8; KEEP: 568.

These statuses count baseline declarations/scenarios, including surviving consolidation owners. They are not claims that every consolidation row is a removed runtime test. Shared-latch and cost-comparison cases are counted at their original declarations; app-config has three pre-existing standalone scenarios.

Static before/after inventory: 77 → 71 test/check files; 627 → 614 named declarations (the latter includes the three pre-existing standalone checks now registered as tests); 11,251 → 11,029 test lines, net −222. Counts use actual file content and source declarations; runtime parameterized subtests are measured separately by the runner.

Consolidation isolation: a file-level teardown restores the complete initial app-store snapshot, waits one event-loop drain, then resets Agent config, boxes, Knowledge/citations, repo identities/icons, saved prompts, secret files and connection diagnostics. Existing suite-scoped setup/teardown remains. No test installs persistent new listeners; the app store’s subscriptions are initialized once by the shared module. Tab maps and current project selection are immutable store updates and are restored along with API/plugins. Independent name-filtered group runs and a reversed top-level group run are prepared to detect hidden coupling.

Static validation completed: TypeScript parse diagnostics for every final owned test file (none), repository-wide non-test seam caller searches, and git diff --check restricted to the owned paths (passed). These are source inspections, not typechecking or runtime test claims.

Focused runtime validation is complete in the root-scheduled slot below. Root owns the whole-suite baseline/final runs, typechecks, build and emitted-web smoke. These focused measurements do not establish a whole-suite or CI speedup.


## Focused runtime validation

All commands used Node 24.22.1 with `TSX_DISABLE_CACHE=1`, process isolation, `--test-concurrency=4`, the existing ten-minute per-test cap, `tsx` and the UI SVG loader. Both A/B versions used native Node assertions/timers; removed legacy assertion/warning loaders were not preloaded. No generic affected-file rerun was added: the root's changed gate already passed those 19 targets before this slot.

Exact orchestration commands executed from the repository root:

```sh
python3 /tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-validate.py
python3 /tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-perf.py
python3 /tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutate.py
```

Each child command ran from `packages/ui` (the frozen baseline's corresponding directory for baseline comparisons), with this exact native prefix:

```sh
TSX_DISABLE_CACHE=1 node --import tsx --import ./test/svg-loader.mjs --test --test-timeout=600000 --test-concurrency=4
```

Correctness/isolation suffixes and results:

| Suffix after the native prefix | Passed cases | Wall seconds |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| `src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 21 | 0.931 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store routes agent-profile events src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 2 | 2.609 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store wires the boxes store src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 5 | 1.960 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store wires the knowledge store\|the Knowledge tab src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 6 | 1.142 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store routes repo identity events src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 2 | 1.052 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store routes saved-prompt events src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 2 | 1.139 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store wires the secret-files store src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 3 | 1.168 |
| `--test-name-pattern=the app store routes daemon announcements src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts` | 1 | 1.229 |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-order/reversed-app-wiring.test.ts` | 21 | 0.812 runner duration; retry wall not separately measured |

Each name pattern is one literal process argument, including its spaces; the Knowledge pattern's vertical bar is a regex alternative. Exact argument arrays and working directories are in `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-validation/results.json`. Every successful run has zero failures, cancellations and skips. Normal order, every former feature group in a fresh process, and all seven top-level groups reversed independently passed; no order dependence was observed.

The first reversed-order attempt failed before executing any test because the temporary directory linked the workspace root's `node_modules`, which does not expose `@orquester/api`. The temporary-only fix links `packages/ui/node_modules` and supplies `{"type":"module"}`; the unchanged reversed cases then all passed. This was a verification-fixture failure, not a product/test failure, and no assertion or repository source was changed to resolve it. The explicit retry command, from `packages/ui`, was:

```sh
TSX_DISABLE_CACHE=1 node --import tsx --import ./test/svg-loader.mjs --test --test-timeout=600000 --test-concurrency=4 /tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-order/reversed-app-wiring.test.ts
```

## Measured consolidation cost

The focused comparison preserves the same 25 public app-event/plugin/tab/diagnostics cases. Baseline arguments were the six original `agent-profile`, `boxes`, `knowledge`, `repo-identities`, `saved-prompts`, and `secret-files` `app-wiring.test.ts` files plus `connection/connection-log.test.ts`. Final arguments were `src/lib/app-wiring.test.ts src/lib/connection/connection-log.test.ts`. The baseline is the frozen `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/baseline-worktree`; final is the main checkout. Each version launched fresh Node processes with the same native prefix, worker cap and tsx-cache setting. Three pairs alternated baseline then final; all six runs passed all 25 cases.

| Pair | Baseline wall s | Final wall s | Baseline child CPU s | Final child CPU s |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 1 | 7.088 | 7.822 | 23.878 | 9.154 |
| 2 | 14.173 | 2.367 | 50.971 | 3.258 |
| 3 | 6.901 | 1.788 | 24.609 | 2.460 |
| Median | 7.088 | 2.367 | 24.609 | 3.258 |

Observed median focused wall time fell by 4.720 s (66.6%); baseline range 6.901–14.173 s, final 1.788–7.822 s. This is a noisy shared-host observation: independent boxes baseline/Go-link work was visible during these runs, and the first pair's final run was slower despite lower child CPU. It establishes that repeated startup work was removed and records the actual measurements; it is not a controlled CI estimate or a claim of a 66.6% overall-suite speedup. No whole-suite, cold-cache or CI extrapolation is made. Child CPU is Python `RUSAGE_CHILDREN` user+system deltas, not CPU usage of the other host processes.

Largest removed setup work: heavy app-store/module initialization falls from seven copies to one; total test processes fall from seven to two because the diagnostics owner now imports only the log module. Meaningful assertions execute in milliseconds while shared UI initialization dominates these targets. The new one-file owner retains all 21 routing cases; the small diagnostics file owns four log cases without importing the full app store. The root report records overall-suite timing and slowest files. Long simulated audio-drift cases remain because two-hour ±100 ppm accumulation uniquely protects the documented bounded latency/recovery contract; shortening the observation horizon without evidence would weaken it. No per-file audio timing was measured in this focused slot.

Raw logs, exact argument arrays, sample CPU/wall measurements and summary are `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-performance/{results.json,summary.json,baseline-1.log,baseline-2.log,baseline-3.log,final-1.log,final-2.log,final-3.log}`. Timing variance warrants the limits above; no flaky behavioral failure was observed.

## Intended-regression negative controls

Five copied-owner mutations ran sequentially outside the repository. Every mutant loaded correctly and failed the intended assertion; none was counted merely for a nonzero loader/fixture result. The same native prefix ran the following exact final path arguments:

| Copied test argument | Mutation and observed assertion | Exit / wall s |
| --- | --- | ---: |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/terminal-keys/terminal-keys.test.ts` | Stop spending armed Ctrl. The second emitted key stayed `0x12` instead of unmodified `null`; combined Ctrl+locked Alt also retained Ctrl incorrectly. | 1 / 0.181 |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/terminal-reset/terminal-reset.test.ts` | Reset immediately. The observed screen wrongly contained both `SNAPSHOT 1` and `SNAPSHOT 2`, rather than replay alone. | 1 / 0.174 |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/subagent-cache-ttl/subagent-cache-ttl.test.ts` | Reverse less/more cost sign. Independent `9.4% less` and held `3.2% less` oracles rejected the wrong savings data. | 1 / 0.208 |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/probe-timeout/diagnosis.logic.test.ts` | Abort at 500 ms rather than the requested 1000 ms. The signal was already aborted at virtual 999 ms, violating the explicit pre-deadline assertion. | 1 / 1.183 |
| `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/ttl-current-choice/subagent-cache-ttl.test.ts` | Always display selected TTL 5m. The current-choice `1h` oracle failed even though comparison prose still contained `1h`, excluding the earlier possible false positive. | 1 / 0.673 |

Mutation commands, timings and assertion logs are `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/logs/`. Production source was never edited for these controls, so no source-restoration step was needed. A reverse-mutation equality check for all five copied owners proved their restored original bytes equal the current repository owner; recorded SHA256 values and equality results are `/tmp/orquester-test-audit/ui-support-mutations/source-integrity.json`. All modified owners and tests remain the intended reviewed cleanup versions. Temporary mutated copies remain outside the repository as evidence. No commits, daemon operations, server startup or build occurred in this slot.
